                        </h5>

                        <button type="button"
                                class="btn-close"
                                @onclick="CloseForm">
                        </button>

                    </div>

                    <div class="modal-body">

                        @if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_formError))
                        {
                            <div class="alert alert-danger">
                                @_formError
                            </div>
                        }

                        <div class="row">

                            <div class="col-md-8 mb-3">

                                <label class="form-label">
                                    عنوان *
                                </label>

                                <InputText class="form-control"
                                           @bind-Value="_title" />

                                @if (_editing)
                                {
                                    <ValidationMessage For="() => _updateModel.Title" />
                                }
                                else
                                {
                                    <ValidationMessage For="() => _createModel.Title" />
                                }

                            </div>

                            <div class="col-md-4 mb-3">

                                <label class="form-label">
                                    ترتیب نمایش
                                </label>

                                <InputNumber class="form-control"
                                             @bind-Value="_order" />

                            </div>

                        </div>


                        <div class="mb-3">

                            <label class="form-label">
                                Slug *
                            </label>

                            <InputText class="form-control"
                                       @bind-Value="_slug" />

                            <div class="form-text">
                                مثال:
                                <code>software-architecture</code>
                            </div>

                        </div>


                        <div class="mb-3">

                            <label class="form-label">
                                توضیحات
                            </label>

                            <InputTextArea class="form-control"
                                           rows="4"
                                           @bind-Value="_description" />

                        </div>


                        <div class="mb-3">

                            <label class="form-label">
                                تصویر کاور
                            </label>

                            <InputText class="form-control"
                                       @bind-Value="_coverImageUrl" />

                        </div>


                        <div class="row">

                            <div class="col-md-6">

                                <div class="form-check form-switch">

                                    <InputCheckbox class="form-check-input"
                                                   @bind-Value="_isPublished" />

                                    <label class="form-check-label">
                                        منتشر شده
                                    </label>

                                </div>

                            </div>

                            <div class="col-md-6">

                                <div class="form-check form-switch">

                                    <InputCheckbox class="form-check-input"
                                                   @bind-Value="_isInEnglish" />

                                    <label class="form-check-label">
                                        دوره انگلیسی است
                                    </label>

                                </div>

                            </div>

                        </div>

                    </div>

                    <div class="modal-footer">

                        <button type="button"
                                class="btn btn-light"
                                disabled="@_processing"
                                @onclick="CloseForm">

                            انصراف

                        </button>

                        <button type="submit"
                                class="btn btn-primary"
                                disabled="@_processing">

                            @if (_processing)
                            {
                                <span>
                                    در حال ذخیره...
                                </span>
                            }
                            else
                            {
                                <span>
                                    ذخیره
                                </span>

@code {

    private readonly List<CourseGridDto> _items = new();

    private bool _loading = true;
    private bool _processing;

    private bool _showForm;
    private bool _editing;

    private string? _message;
    private string? _formError;

    private bool _isError;

    private int _page = 1;
    private const int PageSize = 20;

    private int _totalItems;
    private int _totalPages;


    private CreateCourseCommand _createModel = new();

    private UpdateCourseCommand _updateModel = new();


    /*
     * این propertyها باعث می‌شوند
     * فرم Create و Edit از یک markup استفاده کند.
     */

    private string _title
    {
        get => _editing
            ? _updateModel.Title
            : _createModel.Title;

        set
        {
            if (_editing)
                _updateModel.Title = value;
            else
                _createModel.Title = value;
        }
    }

    private string _slug
    {
        get => _editing
            ? _updateModel.Slug
            : _createModel.Slug;

        set
        {
            if (_editing)
                _updateModel.Slug = value;
            else
                _createModel.Slug = value;
        }
    }

    private string? _description
    {
        get => _editing
            ? _updateModel.Description
            : _createModel.Description;

        set
        {
            if (_editing)
                _updateModel.Description = value;
            else
                _createModel.Description = value;
        }
    }

    private string? _coverImageUrl
    {
        get => _editing
            ? _updateModel.CoverImageUrl
            : _createModel.CoverImageUrl;

        set
        {
            if (_editing)
                _updateModel.CoverImageUrl = value;
            else
                _createModel.CoverImageUrl = value;
        }
    }

    private bool _isPublished
    {
        get => _editing
            ? _updateModel.IsPublished
            : _createModel.IsPublished;

        set
        {
            if (_editing)
                _updateModel.IsPublished = value;
            else
                _createModel.IsPublished = value;
        }
    }

    private bool _isInEnglish
    {
        get => _editing
            ? _updateModel.IsInEnglish
            : _createModel.IsInEnglish;

        set
        {
            if (_editing)
                _updateModel.IsInEnglish = value;
            else
                _createModel.IsInEnglish = value;
        }
    }

    private int _order
    {
        get => _editing
            ? _updateModel.OrderInCourses
            : _createModel.OrderInCourses;

        set
        {
            if (_editing)
                _updateModel.OrderInCourses = value;
            else
                _createModel.OrderInCourses = value;
        }
    }


    private object _form =>
        _editing
            ? _updateModel
            : _createModel;


    protected override async Task OnInitializedAsync()
    {
        await LoadPage(1);
    }


    private async Task LoadPage(int page)
    {
        _loading = true;
        _message = null;

        try
        {
            GridDataSourceResult<CourseGridDto> result = await GridHandler.Handle(
                new GetCourseListAsGridQuery
                {
                    Page = page,
                    PageSize = PageSize
                },
                CancellationToken.None);

            _items.Clear();

            if (result.Data is not null)
                _items.AddRange(result.Data);

            _page = page;

            _totalItems = result.Total;

            _totalPages = (int)Math.Ceiling(
                (double)_totalItems / PageSize);
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            ShowError(ex.Message);
        }
        finally
        {
            _loading = false;
        }
    }


    private void ShowCreate()
    {
        _editing = false;

        _createModel = new CreateCourseCommand
        {
            OrderInCourses = 0,
            IsPublished = false,
            IsInEnglish = false
        };

        _formError = null;
        _message = null;

        _showForm = true;
    }


    private void ShowEdit(CourseGridDto item)
    {
        _editing = true;

        _updateModel = new UpdateCourseCommand
        {
            Id = item.Id,
            Title = item.Title,
            Slug = item.Slug,
            Description = item.Description,
            CoverImageUrl = item.CoverImageUrl,
            IsPublished = item.IsPublished,
            IsInEnglish = item.IsInEnglish,
            OrderInCourses = item.OrderInCourses
        };

        _formError = null;
        _message = null;

        _showForm = true;
    }


    private void CloseForm()
    {
        if (_processing)
            return;

        _showForm = false;
        _formError = null;
    }


    private async Task Save()
    {
        _processing = true;
        _formError = null;
        _message = null;

        try
        {
            if (_editing)
            {
                await UpdateHandler.Handle(
                    _updateModel,
                    CancellationToken.None);

                ShowSuccess(
                    "دوره با موفقیت ویرایش شد.");
            }
            else
            {
                await CreateHandler.Handle(
                    _createModel,
                    CancellationToken.None);

                ShowSuccess(
                    "دوره با موفقیت ایجاد شد.");
            }

            _showForm = false;

            await LoadPage(_page);
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            _formError = ex.Message;
        }
        finally
        {
            _processing = false;
        }
    }


    private async Task Delete(CourseGridDto item)
    {
        if (_processing)
            return;

        _processing = true;
        _message = null;

        try
        {
            await DeleteHandler.Handle(
                new DeleteCourseCommand
                {
                    Id = item.Id
                },
                CancellationToken.None);

            ShowSuccess(
                "دوره با موفقیت حذف شد.");

            if (_items.Count == 1 && _page > 1)
                _page--;

            await LoadPage(_page);
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            ShowError(ex.Message);
        }
        finally
        {
            _processing = false;
        }
    }


    private void ShowSuccess(string message)
    {
        _message = message;
        _isError = false;
    }


    private void ShowError(string message)
    {
        _message = message;
        _isError = true;
    }

} *@

@code {
    private bool _authorized;

    protected override async Task OnInitializedAsync()
    {
        var authState = await AuthState.GetAuthenticationStateAsync();
        var user = authState.User;

        // چک می‌کنیم کاربر لاگین باشد
        if (user.Identity?.IsAuthenticated == true)
        {
            _authorized = true;
        }
        else
        {
            // هدایت به صفحه لاگین
            Navigation.NavigateTo("/admin/login", forceLoad: true);
        }
    }
}

@rendermode InteractiveServer
@using AppServices.Admin.Posts.Create
@using AppServices.Admin.Lookups
@using AppServices.Base
@using Utilities.Dtos
@inject ICommandHandler<CreatePostCommand, int> CreateHandler

                                   checked="@_selectedTagIds.Contains(t.Id)"
                                   @onchange="e => ToggleTag(t.Id, (bool)(e.Value ?? false))" />

                        </div>

                    @if (_tags.Count == 0)
                    {
                        <span class="text-muted small">برچسبی تعریف نشده.</span>

            @(_saving ? "در حال ذخیره..." : "ذخیره")

@code {
    private CreatePostCommand _model = new() { IsCommentsEnabled = true };
    private List<IdTitleDto<int>> _categories = new();
    private List<IdTitleDto<int>> _tags = new();
    private HashSet<int> _selectedTagIds = new();
    private bool _saving;
    private string? _error;

    protected override async Task OnInitializedAsync()
    {
        _categories = await CategoriesLookup.Handle(new GetCategoriesLookupQuery(), CancellationToken.None);
        _tags = await TagsLookup.Handle(new GetTagsLookupQuery(), CancellationToken.None);
    }

    private void ToggleTag(int id, bool selected)
    {
        if (selected) _selectedTagIds.Add(id);
        else _selectedTagIds.Remove(id);
    }

    private async Task Submit()
    {
        _saving = true;
        _error = null;
        try
        {
            _model.TagIds = _selectedTagIds.ToList();
            var id = await CreateHandler.Handle(_model, CancellationToken.None);
            Nav.NavigateTo($"/admin/posts/edit/{id}");
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            _error = ex.Message;
        }
        finally
        {
            _saving = false;
        }
    }
}

                    <ValidationMessage For="() => _model.Slug" />
                </div>
                <div class="col-12">
                    <label class="form-label">خلاصه *</label>
                    <InputTextArea class="form-control" rows="2" @bind-Value="_model.Summary" />
                    <ValidationMessage For="() => _model.Summary" />
                </div>
                <div class="col-12">
                    <label class="form-label">محتوا (HTML) *</label>
                    <InputTextArea class="form-control font-monospace" rows="12" @bind-Value="_model.Content" dir="auto" />
                    <ValidationMessage For="() => _model.Content" />
                </div>
                <div class="col-md-6">
                    <label class="form-label">دسته‌بندی *</label>
                    <InputSelect class="form-select" @bind-Value="_model.CategoryId">
                        <option value="0">انتخاب کنید...</option>
                        @foreach (var c in _categories)
                        {
                            <option value="@c.Id">@c.Title</option>
                        }
                    </InputSelect>
                </div>
                <div class="col-md-6">
                    <label class="form-label">آدرس تصویر کاور</label>
                    <InputText class="form-control" @bind-Value="_model.CoverImageUrl" dir="ltr" />
                </div>
                <div class="col-md-6">
                    <label class="form-label">Meta Title</label>
                    <InputText class="form-control" @bind-Value="_model.MetaTitle" />
                </div>
                <div class="col-md-6">
                    <label class="form-label">Meta Description</label>
                    <InputText class="form-control" @bind-Value="_model.MetaDescription" />
                </div>
                <div class="col-12">
                    <label class="form-label">برچسب‌ها</label>
                    <div class="d-flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                        @foreach (var t in _tags)
                        {
                            <div class="form-check">
                                <input class="form-check-input" type="checkbox" id="etag-@t.Id"
                                       checked="@_selectedTagIds.Contains(t.Id)"
                                       @onchange="e => ToggleTag(t.Id, (bool)(e.Value ?? false))" />
                                <label class="form-check-label" for="etag-@t.Id">@t.Title</label>
                            </div>
                        }
                    </div>
                </div>
                <div class="col-12">
                    <div class="form-check form-check-inline">
                        <InputCheckbox class="form-check-input" @bind-Value="_model.IsPublished" id="epub" />
                        <label class="form-check-label" for="epub">منتشر شود</label>
                    </div>
                    <div class="form-check form-check-inline">
                        <InputCheckbox class="form-check-input" @bind-Value="_model.IsCommentsEnabled" id="ecmt" />
                        <label class="form-check-label" for="ecmt">نظرات فعال</label>
                    </div>
                    <div class="form-check form-check-inline">
                        <InputCheckbox class="form-check-input" @bind-Value="_model.IsInEnglish" id="een" />
                        <label class="form-check-label" for="een">انگلیسی</label>
                    </div>
                </div>
                <div class="col-12">
                    <small class="text-muted">بازدید: @_viewCount | TinyUrl: @_tinyUrl</small>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
        <div class="card-footer d-flex gap-2">
            <button type="submit" class="btn btn-primary" disabled="@_saving">
                @(_saving ? "در حال ذخیره..." : "ذخیره تغییرات")
            </button>
            <a href="/admin/posts" class="btn btn-light">انصراف</a>
        </div>
    </EditForm>
}

@code {
    [Parameter] public int Id { get; set; }

    private UpdatePostCommand? _model;
    private List<IdTitleDto<int>> _categories = new();
    private List<IdTitleDto<int>> _tags = new();
    private HashSet<int> _selectedTagIds = new();
    private bool _loading = true;
    private bool _saving;
    private string? _error;
    private string? _success;
    private int _viewCount;
    private string _tinyUrl = "";

    protected override async Task OnParametersSetAsync()
    {
        _loading = true;
        _error = null;
        _success = null;
        try
        {
            _categories = await CategoriesLookup.Handle(new GetCategoriesLookupQuery(), CancellationToken.None);
            _tags = await TagsLookup.Handle(new GetTagsLookupQuery(), CancellationToken.None);

            var post = await GetHandler.Handle(new GetPostQuery { Id = Id }, CancellationToken.None);
            _model = new UpdatePostCommand
            {
                Id = post.Id,
                Title = post.Title,
                Slug = post.Slug,
                Summary = post.Summary,
                Content = post.Content,
                CoverImageUrl = post.CoverImageUrl,
                CategoryId = post.CategoryId,
                IsPublished = post.IsPublished,
                IsCommentsEnabled = post.IsCommentsEnabled,
                IsInEnglish = post.IsInEnglish,
                MetaTitle = post.MetaTitle,
                MetaDescription = post.MetaDescription,
                OgImageUrl = post.OgImageUrl,
                TagIds = post.TagIds
            };
            _selectedTagIds = post.TagIds.ToHashSet();
            _viewCount = post.ViewCount;
            _tinyUrl = post.TinyUrl;
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            _model = null;
            _error = ex.Message;
        }
        finally
        {
            _loading = false;
        }
    }

    private void ToggleTag(int id, bool selected)
    {
        if (selected) _selectedTagIds.Add(id);
        else _selectedTagIds.Remove(id);
    }

    private async Task Submit()
    {
        if (_model is null) return;
        _saving = true;
        _error = null;
        _success = null;
        try
        {
            _model.TagIds = _selectedTagIds.ToList();
            await UpdateHandler.Handle(_model, CancellationToken.None);
            _success = "تغییرات ذخیره شد.";
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            _error = ex.Message;
        }
        finally
        {
            _saving = false;
        }
    }
}

@code {
    private List<PostGridDto> _items = new();
    private bool _loading = true;
    private bool _deleting;
    private int _page = 1;
    private int _pageSize = 15;
    private int _totalPages;
    private int _totals;
    private string? _message;
    private bool _isError;

    protected override async Task OnInitializedAsync() => await LoadPage(1);

    private async Task LoadPage(int page)
    {
        _loading = true;
        _page = page;
        try
        {
            var result = await GridHandler.Handle(new GetPostListAsGridQuery
            {
                Page = _page,
                PageSize = _pageSize
            }, CancellationToken.None);

            _items = result.Data?.ToList() ?? new();
            _totalPages = result.TotalPages;
            _totals = result.Total;
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            _message = ex.Message;
            _isError = true;
            _items = new();
        }
        finally
        {
            _loading = false;
        }
    }

    private async Task ConfirmDelete(PostGridDto item)
    {
        if (!await Task.FromResult(true)) return; // UI confirm simplified
        _deleting = true;
        try
        {
            await DeleteHandler.Handle(new DeletePostCommand { Id = item.Id }, CancellationToken.None);
            _message = $"مطلب «{item.Title}» حذف شد.";
            _isError = false;
            await LoadPage(_page);
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            _message = ex.Message;
            _isError = true;
        }
        finally
        {
            _deleting = false;
        }
    }
}


=== FILE: src/Web/Components/Admin/Pages/Tags.razor ===
@* @page "/admin/tags"
@layout Web.Components.Admin.Layout.AdminLayout
@rendermode InteractiveServer

@using AppServices.Admin.Tags.Create
@using AppServices.Admin.Tags.Update
@using AppServices.Admin.Tags.Delete
@using AppServices.Admin.Tags.Grid
@using AppServices.Base
@using PersonalBlog.Domain.Entities.Tags.Dtos

@inject IQueryHandler<GetTagListAsGridQuery, GridDataSourceResult<TagGridDto>> GridHandler
@inject ICommandHandler<CreateTagCommand, int> CreateHandler
@inject ICommandHandler<UpdateTagCommand, int> UpdateHandler
@inject ICommandHandler<DeleteTagCommand, int> DeleteHandler

<PageTitle>برچسب‌ها | ادمین</PageTitle>

<div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-4">

@code {

    private List<TagGridDto> _items = new();

    private bool _loading = true;
    private bool _processing;

    private bool _showForm;
    private bool _editing;

    private string? _message;
    private bool _isError;

    private int _page = 1;
    private int _pageSize = 20;

    private int _totalItems;
    private int _totalPages;

    private CreateTagCommand _createModel = new();

    private UpdateTagCommand _updateModel = new();

    private object _form => _editing
        ? _updateModel
        : _createModel;

    protected override async Task OnInitializedAsync()
    {
        await LoadPage(1);
    }

    private async Task LoadPage(int page)
    {
        _loading = true;
        _message = null;

        try
        {
            GridDataSourceResult<TagGridDto> result = await GridHandler.Handle(
                new GetTagListAsGridQuery
                {
                    Page = page,
                    PageSize = _pageSize
                },
                CancellationToken.None);

            _items = result.Data?.ToList() ?? new();

            _page = page;

            _totalItems = result.Totals;

            _totalPages = (int)Math.Ceiling(
                (double)_totalItems / _pageSize);
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            _items = new();
            ShowError(ex.Message);
        }
        finally
        {
            _loading = false;
        }
    }

    private void ShowCreate()
    {
        _editing = false;

        _createModel = new CreateTagCommand();

        _message = null;

        _showForm = true;
    }

    private void ShowEdit(TagGridDto item)
    {
        _editing = true;

        _updateModel = new UpdateTagCommand
        {
            Id = item.Id,
            Title = item.Title
        };

        _message = null;

        _showForm = true;
    }

    private void CloseForm()
    {
        if (_processing)
            return;

        _showForm = false;
    }

    private async Task Save()
    {
        _processing = true;
        _message = null;

        try
        {
            if (_editing)
            {
                await UpdateHandler.Handle(
                    _updateModel,
                    CancellationToken.None);
            }
            else
            {
                await CreateHandler.Handle(
                    _createModel,
                    CancellationToken.None);
            }

            _showForm = false;

            ShowSuccess(
                _editing
                    ? "برچسب با موفقیت ویرایش شد."
                    : "برچسب با موفقیت ایجاد شد.");

            await LoadPage(_page);
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            ShowError(ex.Message);
        }
        finally
        {
            _processing = false;
        }
    }

    private async Task Delete(TagGridDto item)
    {
        if (_processing)
            return;

        _processing = true;
        _message = null;

        try
        {
            await DeleteHandler.Handle(
                new DeleteTagCommand
                {
                    Id = item.Id
                },
                CancellationToken.None);

            ShowSuccess("برچسب با موفقیت حذف شد.");

            if (_items.Count == 1 && _page > 1)
                _page--;

            await LoadPage(_page);
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            ShowError(ex.Message);
        }
        finally
        {
            _processing = false;
        }
    }

    private void ShowSuccess(string message)
    {
        _message = message;
        _isError = false;
    }

    private void ShowError(string message)
    {
        _message = message;
        _isError = true;
    }
} *@

<div class="site-wrapper @(_isDark ? "dark" : "")" data-theme="@(_isDark ? "dark" : "light")">

@code {
    private bool _isDark;
    private bool _menuOpen;

    protected override async Task OnAfterRenderAsync(bool firstRender)
    {
        if (firstRender)
        {
            try
            {
                var theme = await JS.InvokeAsync<string>("eval", "localStorage.getItem('theme') || 'light'");
                _isDark = theme == "dark";
                await ApplyThemeClass();
                StateHasChanged();
            }
            catch { }
        }
    }

    private async Task ToggleTheme()
    {
        _isDark = !_isDark;
        await JS.InvokeVoidAsync("eval", $"localStorage.setItem('theme', '{(_isDark ? "dark" : "light")}')");
        await ApplyThemeClass();
    }

    private async Task ApplyThemeClass()
    {
        await JS.InvokeVoidAsync("eval", _isDark
            ? "document.documentElement.classList.add('dark')"
            : "document.documentElement.classList.remove('dark')");
    }

    private void ToggleMenu() => _menuOpen = !_menuOpen;
    private void CloseMenu() => _menuOpen = false;
}

@page "/Error"
@using System.Diagnostics

<PageTitle>Error</PageTitle>

<h1 class="text-danger">Error.</h1>
<h2 class="text-danger">An error occurred while processing your request.</h2>

@if (ShowRequestId)
{
    <p>
        <strong>Request ID:</strong> <code>@RequestId</code>
    </p>
}

<h3>Development Mode</h3>
<p>
    Swapping to <strong>Development</strong> environment will display more detailed information about the error that occurred.
</p>
<p>
    <strong>The Development environment shouldn't be enabled for deployed applications.</strong>
    It can result in displaying sensitive information from exceptions to end users.
    For local debugging, enable the <strong>Development</strong> environment by setting the <strong>ASPNETCORE_ENVIRONMENT</strong> environment variable to <strong>Development</strong>
    and restarting the app.
</p>

@code{
    [CascadingParameter]
    private HttpContext? HttpContext { get; set; }

    private string? RequestId { get; set; }
    private bool ShowRequestId => !string.IsNullOrEmpty(RequestId);

    protected override void OnInitialized() =>
        RequestId = Activity.Current?.Id ?? HttpContext?.TraceIdentifier;
}

@page "/"
@page "/home"
@layout Web.Components.Site.Layout.SiteLayout
@rendermode InteractiveServer
@using AppServices.Site.Posts
@using AppServices.Base
@inject IQueryHandler<GetLatestPostsQuery, List<GetLatestPostsResult>> LatestPostsHandler

<PageTitle>خانه | وبلاگ شخصی</PageTitle>

<section class="mb-8">
    <h1 class="text-2xl md:text-3xl font-bold mb-2">آخرین مطالب</h1>
    <p class="text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-400 text-sm">جدیدترین نوشته‌های وبلاگ</p>
</section>

@if (_loading)
{
    <div class="space-y-4">
        @for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++)
        {
            <div class="animate-pulse bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 rounded-2xl p-5 shadow-sm">
                <div class="h-4 bg-gray-200 dark:bg-gray-700 rounded w-3/4 mb-3"></div>
                <div class="h-3 bg-gray-200 dark:bg-gray-700 rounded w-full mb-2"></div>
                <div class="h-3 bg-gray-200 dark:bg-gray-700 rounded w-5/6"></div>
            </div>
        }
    </div>
}
else if (_posts.Count == 0)
{
    <div class="text-center py-12 text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">
        هنوز مطلبی منتشر نشده است.
    </div>
}
else
{
    <div class="space-y-4">
        @foreach (var post in _posts)
        {
            <article class="bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 rounded-2xl p-5 shadow-sm border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-700 hover:shadow-md transition">
                <a href="/post/@post.Slug" class="block">
                    <div class="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 mb-2">
                        <span class="bg-primary-50 dark:bg-primary-900/30 text-primary-700 dark:text-primary-300 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">@post.CategoryTitle</span>
                        @if (post.PublishedAt.HasValue)
                        {
                            <span>@post.PublishedAt.Value.ToString("yyyy/MM/dd")</span>
                        }
                    </div>
                    <h2 class="text-lg font-semibold mb-2 text-gray-900 dark:text-white leading-snug">@post.Title</h2>
                    <p class="text-sm text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-300 line-clamp-2">@post.Summary</p>
                    <div class="mt-3 flex items-center text-xs text-gray-400">
                        <span>@post.ViewCount بازدید</span>
                    </div>
                </a>
            </article>
        }
    </div>
}

@code {
    private List<GetLatestPostsResult> _posts = new();
    private bool _loading = true;

    protected override async Task OnInitializedAsync()
    {
        try
        {
            _posts = await LatestPostsHandler.Handle(new GetLatestPostsQuery { Count = 10 }, CancellationToken.None);
        }
        catch
        {
            _posts = new();
        }
        finally
        {
            _loading = false;
        }
    }
}

@page "/post/{Slug}"
@layout Web.Components.Site.Layout.SiteLayout
@rendermode InteractiveServer
@using AppServices.Site.Posts
@using AppServices.Base
@inject IQueryHandler<GetPostBySlugQuery, GetPostBySlugResult> PostHandler

<PageTitle>@(_post?.Title ?? "مطالب") | وبلاگ شخصی</PageTitle>

@if (_loading)
{
    <div class="animate-pulse space-y-4">
        <div class="h-8 bg-gray-200 dark:bg-gray-700 rounded w-3/4"></div>
        <div class="h-4 bg-gray-200 dark:bg-gray-700 rounded w-1/2"></div>
        <div class="h-40 bg-gray-200 dark:bg-gray-700 rounded"></div>
    </div>
}
else if (_post is null)
{
    <div class="text-center py-16">
        <p class="text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">مطلب یافت نشد.</p>
        <a href="/" class="text-sky-600 mt-4 inline-block">بازگشت به خانه</a>
    </div>
}
else
{
    <article>
        <div class="mb-4 flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">
            <a href="/" class="hover:text-sky-600">خانه</a>
            <span>/</span>
            <span>@_post.CategoryTitle</span>
        </div>

        <h1 class="text-2xl md:text-3xl font-bold mb-3 leading-tight">@_post.Title</h1>

        <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 text-xs text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 mb-6">
            @if (_post.PublishedAt.HasValue)
            {
                <span>@_post.PublishedAt.Value.ToString("yyyy/MM/dd")</span>
            }
            <span>•</span>
            <span>@_post.ViewCount بازدید</span>
        </div>

        @if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_post.CoverImageUrl))
        {
            <img src="@_post.CoverImageUrl" alt="@_post.Title" class="w-full rounded-2xl mb-6 object-cover max-h-80" />
        }

        <div class="prose prose-sm dark:prose-invert max-w-none mb-8 leading-relaxed">
            @((MarkupString)_post.Content)
        </div>

        @if (_post.Tags.Any())
        {
            <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2 mb-8">
                @foreach (var tag in _post.Tags)
                {
                    <span class="px-3 py-1 text-xs rounded-full bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-700 text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300">#@tag</span>
                }
            </div>
        }
    </article>
}

@code {
    [Parameter] public string Slug { get; set; } = default!;

    private GetPostBySlugResult? _post;
    private bool _loading = true;

    protected override async Task OnParametersSetAsync()
    {
        _loading = true;
        try
        {
            _post = await PostHandler.Handle(new GetPostBySlugQuery { Slug = Slug }, CancellationToken.None);
        }
        catch
        {
            _post = null;
        }
        finally
        {
            _loading = false;
        }
    }
}
